# Pattern index (MSVC 5.0 /O2 — Gruntz)

One line per pattern: `- [title](file.md) — cN — tags — symptoms` (cN = confidence /10).
Sorted by primary tag so families cluster. Grep this file by tag (`cpp:switch`,
`cpp:eh`, `topic:wall`) or by symptom token, then read only the hits. Schema +
how-to-search: [`README.md`](README.md). Authoring: new pattern = new file + one
line here, SAME commit.

WALL vs STEERABLE: `topic:codegen-idiom`/`topic:flags` = a source spelling closes the
diff; `topic:wall`/`topic:scoring-artifact` = code already correct / no source form —
stop chasing (orchestration §2a).

READING OLD TOOL NAMES. Many symptom lines below name the instrument that first
measured the pattern. Several of those are retired: the `sema disasm` view flags
(`--diff`, `--branches`, `--blocks`, `--rich`, `--base`) and the tree-wide sieves
(`jcc_sieve`, `insn_count`, `base_size`, `immediates`, `store_offsets`,
`gruntz verify assert-relocs`, `inline_clones`, `exit_merge_sieve`, `shrink_wrap`, …). The
measurements stand; the commands do not. The live per-function successor is
**`gruntz walls diagnose <rva>`** — it prints both sides' byte length, instruction,
call, branch, `ret` and relocation counts, names the divergence class, and dumps
the unmasked pair with `--asm`. The tree-wide worklist is `gruntz walls inventory`.
Full old→new table: [`../tooling-map.md`](../tooling-map.md).

- [Direct four-word rectangle copies distinguish RECT from CRect assignment](plain-rect-vs-crect-assignment.md) - c10 - cpp:global cpp:struct msvc5:mfc | topic:data topic:codegen-idiom topic:correctness - VC5 compiles both CRect assignment forms through imported CopyRect, while plain `RECT = *RECT*` emits retail's direct four-load/four-store sequence

- [A struct-shaped spill pattern says `T r = *p;`, but only where the frame is the ONLY residue](whole-struct-copy-vs-scalars.md) - c8 - cpp:struct cpp:local cpp:temporary | asm:mov asm:sub | topic:codegen-idiom topic:wall - only `sub esp,N` and a few `[esp+M]` differ, and retail's spill slots for a rectangle's fields are spaced by the STRUCT stride (8 or 12 apart) instead of packed, or an unread field is written anyway. cl 5.0 gives a struct local its whole size at real field offsets; four scalars get only the dwords it actually spills, packed. `RECT lr = *src;` plus a block scope took CPlay::DrawDebugStatsFull 0xcf0a0 94.72 -> 99.998 (frame 0x2a8 -> 0x298); its remaining one dead spill chooses a different reusable slot and stayed flat across 75 successfully compiled local/TU-state variants. CPlay::OnLButtonDblClk 0xce660 moved 95.70 -> 98.65. BUT THE FRAME IS NOT THE ARBITER - the instruction COUNT is: CPlay::OnKeyDown 0xcbcc0 shows the same signal at both of its bounds tests and the change is wrong, 90.42 -> 85.95, because the struct form keeps more of the rect in registers and sheds 33 instructions (1957 vs retail's 1993, in-order agreement 47.5% -> 33.5%) while the scalar form's count is retail's to within three. Measure agreement over the whole function before taking a frame-size argument. Not applicable to 8-byte `SIZE` (two fields = no spacing to read): ClampViewport2 91.27 -> 89.72
- [A struct-copy local that retail STORES and we fold is a register-pressure reading, not a different source shape](aggregate-copy-materializes-only-under-register-pressure.md) - c8 - cpp:struct cpp:local | asm:mov | topic:wall topic:regalloc - retail writes an 8-byte aggregate local to the frame beside the very values it pushes as arguments and nothing reads the stores; ours pushes only. cl 5.0 scalar-replaces a small aggregate copy ONLY while registers are free - copy-init, assignment, field-by-field, a class type with a member function and an inline-helper body are all byte-identical (folded), while the same copy plus six live values across two calls materializes. Not source-reachable: the stores mean retail has MORE live values there than your body does. Converse is the useful direction. WanderStep 0xed9f0, three sites
- [Adjacent live coordinate pairs are `Coord` aggregates, not independent scalars](adjacent-live-coordinate-pairs-are-coord-aggregates.md) - c10 - cpp:struct cpp:local cpp:copy | asm:mov asm:sub | topic:codegen-idiom topic:eh - retail loads or derives both halves of one position, stores them at adjacent `base+0`/`base+4` homes, and repeatedly consumes both as that point; independent scalars land in unrelated homes. `CGrunt::PathScan` closes both unwind funclets. Negative control: `StepCompassMove` has adjacent field-wise traffic but no complete-object call/copy, and the aggregate model is disproven. Unlike a dead 8-byte spill, both fields must be read as one evidenced identity.

- [A `static char s_X[] = "lit"` declared in TWO TUs is ONE pooled literal](duplicate-char-array-is-a-pooled-literal.md) - c10 - cpp:global cpp:string msvc5:gf | topic:data topic:correctness - `gruntz verify assert-relocs` shows our base naming a static where the delinked target names another TU's static or a bare `??_C@` literal, at the same place, while the masked diff shows nothing (both sides push a DIR32 address; only the relocation's NAME differs). `/O2` implies `/Gf`, so retail has exactly ONE address per distinct literal and a file-static cannot be the thing two TUs share - the duplicate declaration is the fabrication. Two decisive tests, neither needing a disassembly read: a bare literal in TU A resolving to TU B's file-static's pinned address, and the `provisional-pooled-literal-alias` rows the delink data manifest already computes. Fix: bare literal everywhere plus ONE `DATA_COMPGEN(rva, "lit")` at a use site in the owning unit. 37 fabricated declarations removed, 3493 -> 3515 exact, `?BuildMainMenuTree@@YAHPAVCChatBox@@H@Z` 99.95 -> 100.00 EXACT on 13 references to one literal; it also killed the `extern char s_codeX[];` block in Grunt.h. Trap: three of them had no use at all, so check another unit claims the address before deleting the last claimant

- [A folded constant index makes the delinker name the NEIGHBOURING global](folded-base-address-names-the-neighbour.md) - c10 - cpp:global cpp:loop | topic:scoring-artifact topic:wall - `gruntz verify assert-relocs` reports "one reference moved to the neighbour" in every function that walks the same array, and the score sits hundredths below 100 with no source change moving it. cl folds a constant index into the address constant: `&buf[N]` becomes the address one past `buf` (the NEXT global's base) and `buf[i - 1]` becomes `buf - 1` (inside the PRECEDING global). Our obj records `buf` plus an addend; the delinker has only the resolved address and names whichever symbol contains it. All six `fileimage` `Decode*` functions show it (six palette buffers exactly 0x400 apart in declaration order); `?BuildSoundFontPath@@YAHD@Z` shows the other direction. Do NOT respell the bound as the neighbour - that fits the delinker's guess instead of what cl emitted. If the neighbour is an unreferenced placeholder with no independent storage evidence, remove the false claim instead: `g_val_24c434` hijacked `g_soundChannelInUse + 0x44` and held `ChannelSlots_FindFree` at 99.5%. `gruntz verify assert-relocs` flags the pair `[folded-base artifact]` from the symbol extents; adjacency IS the proof. The data denominator now uses candidate/retail relocation pairing to stop one-past array and one-past member pointers from inventing eligible data after an enrolled array. THREE shapes, all one mechanism (2026-08-11): ADJACENT, possibly through a CHAIN - three back-to-back arrays each walked to their end make the MIDDLE one gain a reference and lose one, so it cancels out of the diff entirely and a one-hop test reports the far end as a defect (three of the five `CBattlezDlg::DoDataExchange` rows); INTERIOR - a field of a global struct, spelled as an anonymous `DAT_` because nothing is defined there (`CNetSession::SendOne` writes g_netCmdSendMsg +1/+5/+9/+0xd/+0xe, `RegistryHelper::Open` copies `char sz[] = "Software"` in three chunks so +4/+8 land inside the literal); BELOW THE BASE - `buf[i-k]` relocates against `buf-k` (g_clut -2, g_mapNameBuf -4). The chain and DAT_ shapes are now flagged; the string-literal one is not, because a pooled literal has no extent in build/gen/bindings.tsv to test containment against
- [A compiler-generated datum the two sides NAME differently caps a 99.8-99.9% row](compiler-generated-datum-names-differ-per-side.md) - c9 - cpp:static cpp:dtor cpp:const | asm:push asm:call | topic:scoring-artifact topic:wall topic:tooling - `walls diagnose` says `REFERENT - masked bytes identical; the relocation TARGETS differ` and the two names are plainly the SAME object. TWO sub-classes no source spelling can close. (A) cl's `atexit` static-destructor thunk emitted as its own COMDAT: base `$anon_data_<sha>` vs target `__ehreg$<fn>` at the `push` before `call atexit` in a function-local-static guard - the thunk is real (a bare `c3` for a POD, `mov ecx,<obj>; jmp <dtor>` for a class); canonicalize renames it only when cl emits it as a `$L` label INSIDE the owning COMDAT, which is why `CButeMgr::SetRect/SetPoint/SetVector` match while `GetRect/GetPoint/GetVector/GetRange/GetString` and `CImage::RenderFrame/RenderFrameClipped` cannot. `RVA_DYNINIT` does not fix it - the claim LOSES, and with no census row `verify unique-names` rejects the pin (`not an admitted census row`). (B) a named static's canonical suffix embeds its physical EXTENT (`span = end - start`, distance to the next DEFINED symbol), so the same datum with the same bytes gets two `$Sdata_rdata_<sha>` names when the two sides disagree about what FOLLOWS it: `CFader::RunFade`/`RunFadeStepped`'s `kMsToSeconds` is 0.001f on both sides (retail 0x1f07bc = `6f 12 83 3a`) but sits in a 0xc FP pool here and a 0xb0 delinker blob there, and `CButeMgr::GetFloat`'s `s_floatErr` likewise. Consequence: every FP-pool user in a TU is COUPLED - one extra pooled constant anywhere (here `CFaderMesh::ApplyInit` at 82% contributes 1.0f and 0.0f) changes the first one's extent, so the row is gated on the whole TU's pool, not on the victim body. Read the retail bytes before believing a value differs
- [A pinned archive relocation recovers an unlabelled library datum](static-library-relocation-recovers-data-identity.md) - c10 - data:rdata data:bss msvc5:coff | asm:mov asm:push | topic:data topic:identity topic:tooling - for an admitted NAFXCW/LIBCMT/LIBCIMT retail function, the pinned archive member retains a DIR32 relocation at `archive-fn-value + (retail-site - retail-fn)`. Its symbol and signed addend recover the original data base; the defining member proves storage and extent. `gruntz verify library-data-refs` applies the join to touched coverage gaps. `operator new` proved `0x1eafbc = __pfnUninitialized$S66856`; the same oracle falsified seven copy-pasted `CByteArray` archive labels and recovered their distinct MFC `CRuntimeClass` records. Ambiguity is reported, never settled by RVA adjacency
- [A pointer-controlled trivial template loop retains its initializer after VC5 deletes the loop](pointer-controlled-trivial-template-loop-retains-initializer.md) - c10 - cpp:template cpp:loop cpp:ctor cpp:dtor | asm:mov asm:push asm:call | topic:codegen-idiom - a trivial PMF specialization loses its element walk but retail retains one dead m_base/m_alloc store; making the element pointer, rather than a numeric counter, the for-loop control variable produces that store naturally and makes the destructor exact

- [A pinned datum's retail SECTION proves its const-ness, and its retail RVA proves its declaration order](pinned-datum-section-and-order.md) - c10 - cpp:global cpp:const | topic:data topic:codegen-idiom - a `.data`/`.rdata` section stuck far below 100 while every payload is present, or one long `DIFF_DELETE` whose bytes reappear in a later `DIFF_INSERT`. cl sends a `const` file-scope definition to `.rdata` and a non-`const` one to `.data`, so a `DATA()` pin in retail `.data` (0x00208000..0x00229400) proves the TU wrote no `const` and a pin in `.rdata` (0x001e7000..0x00207fa8) proves it did; the wrong one puts the whole payload in a section the delinked target does not have. Separately the delinker packs a unit's ordinary data in ascending retail RVA while cl emits it in declaration order, so ascending RVA IS the original declaration order and sorting the pins recovers it (gruntentrancemove .data 37.6 -> 88.3, mainmenubuilder 47.4 -> 89.3). Traps: a namespace-scope `const` is internal-linkage (needs `extern const` in the owning header) and is DISCARDED when its own TU has no reader, which silently drops the pin (`labels ... MISS <rva> data candidate not in base obj`)
- [Two dword reads at +0 and +4 with no `fld` is an 8-byte constant COPY, not a layout bug](eight-byte-constant-copied-as-a-dword-pair.md) - c10 - cpp:global cpp:const | asm:mov | topic:data topic:codegen-idiom - a `const double` global that the x87 unit NEVER touches, read only by `mov r32,DWORD PTR ds:X` and `mov r32,DWORD PTR ds:X+4`, so a per-offset access map reads the `+4` load as a second field living inside the declared `double`. cl 5.0 copies an 8-byte scalar constant into a member with two dword moves, never `fld`/`fstp`. The copy shows the SAME access count at `+0` and `+4`, all width 4, all reads and no x87 access on the datum; a genuine two-field object differs in count, width or direction. Calibration pair ?g_movingLogicMin@@3NB / ?g_movingLogicMax@@3NB (0x1f04b0, 0x1f04b8), 13 reads each side, from the CProjectile/CMotionState/CMovingLogic/CGrunt ctors - both `double`s are correct. `gruntz verify data-access` counts it as `width-skip-dword-pair`
- [A member store sandwiched between the NEXT call's pushes holds the PREVIOUS call's result](store-between-pushes-belongs-to-the-previous-call.md) - c10 - cpp:member cpp:local cpp:method | asm:mov asm:push asm:call | topic:codegen-idiom topic:identity - back-to-back calls whose results you must attribute: cl 5.0 /O2 hoists the `mov [this+N],eax` store into the argument-setup of the FOLLOWING call, so it reads as that call's result. Read it by DATA FLOW - eax is still the previous call's return value. The corroborating oracle is the DEFAULT ARGUMENT: a settings probe passes the member's current value as its own default, so `push [this+N]` before the call and `mov [this+N],eax` after it name the SAME member; pairing one probe's default with one member and its result with another is the bug. Second oracle: a local computed and never read (`clang -Wunused-variable` over the clangd compile DB, 22 rows across 339 TUs) - retail stored that value somewhere. CGruntzMgr::Run 0x83450 had `m_isEasyMode = resolutionRaw`, so m_isEasyMode took the resolution index (never 0) and the game ran permanently in EASY MODE - secret teleporter triggers self-destructed, static hazards never pulsed, KitchenSlime never grabbed, water/death bridge tiles were downgraded at load. 82.96 -> 83.17, registry block now byte-identical
- [Outer guard on a LOCAL + inner re-test on the MEMBER survives cl's branch folding](local-guard-member-retest-blocks-fold.md) - c8 - cpp:local cpp:member cpp:branch | asm:cmp asm:jcc | topic:codegen-idiom - a no-call arm missing 1-2 branches vs retail while the post-call arm matches: cl folds member-under-member dominated re-tests AND copy-propagates local-under-local back to members, but cannot connect a LOCAL outer guard to a MEMBER inner re-test - the mixed spelling survives as `cmp <reg>,<zeroreg>`, retail's shape. WanderStep 0xed9f0 83.38 -> 84.34; ScanNearestTarget carries the same shape; screen the whole CGrunt five-store battery family
- [A compound guard before `else if` keeps the failed-conjunct re-test](compound-guard-elseif-keeps-failed-conjunct-retest.md) - c10 - cpp:branch cpp:if | asm:test asm:jcc asm:jmp | topic:codegen-idiom topic:correctness - retail re-tests A only after the first arm's B conjunct fails, but a successful first arm jumps over that re-test: only `if (A && B) ... else if (!A && B)` has both edges. Nesting deletes the re-test; sibling `if`s delete the skip-jump. SyncChannelSlot closes 10/11 -> 11/11 branches and moves CFG -> REGALLOC
- [Global reload runs prove scoped pointer locals](global-reload-runs-prove-scoped-pointer-locals.md) - c9 - cpp:local cpp:pointer cpp:scope | asm:mov asm:idiv | topic:codegen-idiom topic:correctness - candidate has more DIR32 references to one global than retail and a frame one dword too small; one cached pointer per retail reload run recovers both the entities and their scopes. StepArrivalDrop 10 -> 7 exact `g_gameReg` relocations, frame 0x4c -> 0x50, 2908 -> 2856 B against retail 2864; putting both branch-arm locals before their `idiv` over-hoists to six and is refuted
- [Two byte-identical dtor COMDATs are named by LINK ORDER, never by their bytes](identical-derived-dtor-comdat-is-named-by-link-order.md) - c10 - cpp:dtor cpp:inline cpp:eh msvc5:mfc | asm:mov asm:call | topic:identity topic:correctness - one mangled dtor name claimed at TWO retail RVAs (name-injectivity / library-overlap gates), the two bodies differing only in the `call ?DeleteObject` rel32. cl 5.0 deletes the most-derived vptr store in an EMPTY derived dtor (the inlined base dtor overwrites `[this]` before any call observes it), so `??1CPen@@UAE@XZ` and `??1CGdiObject@@UAE@XZ` are byte- AND reloc-identical - the image legitimately holds both, and only COMDAT selection order distinguishes them. Read the EDGES: the base's own vtable pins the base copy (`??_7CGdiObject@@6B@+4 -> ??_G -> ??1`, dialogs 0x016460); a second copy whose ONLY referrer is a `lea ecx,[ebp-N]; jmp <copy>` unwind funclet is the DERIVED dtor of that function's local. fontconfig 0x0220f0 is ??1CPen (MeasureLabel's `CPen pen`), 100.0000 as RVA_COMPGEN, and it named the last `$gap_` referent image-wide (`__ehunwindmap$??1CPen@@UAE@XZ` 0x1f97c8)
- [A TU's COFF section ORDER homes an uncarved body - bracket it in .text AND .data at once](obj-section-order-homes-an-uncarved-body.md) - c9 - cpp:global cpp:string cpp:template msvc5:gy msvc5:gf | topic:identity topic:layout topic:data - a dead body in a functions-census hole and no band table says which TU owns it. cl 5.0's obj section order is fixed and the linker preserves it, so ONE obj gives two independent brackets: the `$E` dynamic-init COMDAT pairs are ALWAYS the first `.text` of their obj (so anything below a unit's `$E` block belongs to an EARLIER obj), each function's pooled `??_C@` literals follow ITS OWN `.text` in REVERSE intern order (right-to-left per call), `??_R0` RTTI comes after the strings, and template instantiations are LAST. The dead `fopen("c:\\foo.log","wb")` pair at 0x0942e0/0x094310 is levelrezpath's: it sits below gruntzwnd's `$E` block (0x094370) in `.text` and its two literals (0x211148/0x211158) continue levelrezpath's string run and stop at gruntzwnd's first `.data` (`??_R0?AVCGameWnd@@@8` 0x211160). `.bss` proves NOTHING - retail's uninitialized globals are COMMON-pooled and interleave units. Both bodies 100.0000
- [A small header inline was transcribed once per call site - find them by clone signature](shared-inline-transcribed-once-per-call-site.md) - c10 - cpp:inline cpp:global cpp:call | asm:imul asm:lea asm:cmp | topic:identity topic:codegen-idiom - the same 8-20 instruction block verbatim in many unrelated functions, or an odd immediate (`0x269ec3`, `0x939`) recurring at unrelated RVAs, or N near-identical `static inline` helpers under N different local names in N `.cpp`s. Whoever reconstructs a TU in isolation sees the EXPANSION, not the source, so a header inline gets open-coded once per site. `gruntz walls inline-model` histograms non-address immediates (the `.reloc` table says which are pointers; a magic multiply's addend hides in a `lea` displacement); `--ngrams -n 8` clusters register-alpha-renamed instruction shingles. Decisive confirmation is the STRING COUNT: under /Gf a literal written at 60 sites occupies 60 addresses, one emitted from a shared header COMDAT-folds to ONE - all nineteen act keys have exactly one address each. Calibration points (already-shared helpers it re-finds): zDArray::ResolveEntry, LOGIC_WORKER_PUMP, CMapMgr::CellFlagsAt. GetRandomNumber 14 copies/11 TUs, act-name registrar 61 copies/34 TUs
- [`if (X) { ... return K; } else { return K; }` IS `||` - the TOTAL exit-merge regime](if-else-both-arms-return-is-the-or-regime.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:if cpp:return cpp:goto | asm:xor asm:jcc asm:ret | topic:codegen-idiom - every `return 0` collapses onto ONE sunk epilogue even though the source uses separate `if (c) return 0;` guards, and no `||` or `&&` greps in the source. A nested-return `if` with an else-arm returning the SAME value is a FOURTH spelling of the TOTAL regime and is byte-for-byte identical to `||` (measured: cells A and C both 2 rets / 63 insns, flat sequential guards 6 rets). Its cost lands on UNRELATED guards far away, so the symptom appears nowhere near the cause. Flatten it, then choose the regime you want - retail's guarded serialise/init functions are PARTIAL, i.e. `goto fail;`. CTriggerMgr::ScanGroup 0x7a760 89.48 -> 99.19 (31/31 blocks, 20/20 branch targets). Also a correctness-bug detector: the same `else` was failing every overlay-less save, where retail skips the Serialize call and falls into the success tail. Screen: `gruntz walls diagnose`
- [OVER-MERGE is decided BEFORE layout: two identical exits merge iff both are `jcc` targets](over-merge-is-decided-before-layout.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:return cpp:if cpp:goto | asm:jcc asm:ret asm:mov | topic:wall topic:codegen-idiom - `exit_merge_sieve --over` on a function whose block skeleton is otherwise exact: retail has one INLINE `return K` reached by fall-through PLUS one sunk copy reached by `jcc`, we have one shared copy. Two measured facts. (1) The merge runs BEFORE the register peephole, so it is not a byte-level cross-jump: adding one throwaway statement to the sunk copy instantly restores retail's `mov [esi+0x98],eax` in the other one (eax proven 0 by the preceding `test`), i.e. the register reuse is a CONSEQUENCE of not merging - no spelling that only changes final ENCODING can reach it. (2) cl merges only when BOTH copies would be `jcc` targets; two identical FALL-THROUGH copies are never merged. So the flat-guard form DOES split the exits (ResolveLinkedObject 0x151b90 4 rets = retail's 4) but cannot also SINK one of them, which is retail's actual arrangement - that is the whole wall. Hoisting the out-param declaration above the guard fixed the argument-setup schedule instead: 74.71 -> 80.59 (flat 62.94, goto-label 59.76). REJECTED, measured byte-identical: nesting an `&&` chain into positive-gate `if`s (CPlay::OnRButtonDown 0xceae0 89.98 -> 89.98, which also settles the regime table's positive-gate row), `else if` chain -> separate `if`s with `return;` (CWwdGameObjectA::BltDirtyEx 0x1506b0 73.77 -> 73.77), local-decl reorder, `rc[0] + w` instead of `ir.left + w`, an explicit trailing `return;`. Downstream of the merge and NOT separately steerable: inverted branch polarity, block reordering that costs the loop its fall-through exit (CProjectile::ScanTargets 0xe0b10), and a `return <reg>` tail with a `mov ebx,1` at every break site (CStatusBarMgr::UpdateStatusBarTabHighlight 0xfe910). BUT `goto fail;` DOES reach part of this bucket, so the mirror is not wholly unsolved: where a single guard's `return K` is parked at the END by retail and emitted inline as the test's fall-through by us, the goto form buys retail's branch polarity - CProjectile::SerializeMove 0xe0d40 94.39 -> 94.82 with B32-B38 going to `==`; the same two guards as `||` score 92.08 (the TOTAL regime also swallows two returns retail keeps inline, so COUNT retail's `return 0` copies first). SIEVE TRAP: `table_stop()` cuts at the last `ret` and a delinked jump-table address whose third byte is 0xc2 decodes as `ret imm16`, so switch functions read one target ret too many - CPlay::LoadWarlordSprites 0xd65d0 `1 -> 2` is a NON-hit
- [`goto fail;` is the ONLY spelling that shares SOME exits and duplicates the rest](goto-fail-shares-one-exit-block.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:goto cpp:return | asm:xor asm:jcc asm:ret | topic:codegen-idiom - base has MORE ret blocks than target (DUP-EXIT): retail's leading guards `je` one shared exit while ours each fall through to their own copy, and the shared block keeps the `xor eax,eax` ours elides. cl 5.0 /O2 has exactly THREE exit-merging regimes chosen by the SOURCE construct - separate `if (c) return 0;` merges NOTHING, `goto fail;` + a trailing `fail: return 0;` merges the goto sites and leaves every other `return 0` its own copy, and `||`/`&&`/a positive-gate nest collapses EVERY same-valued return in the whole function into one sunk block. Nothing else moves them: not /GX, body size, jump distance or statement order. The elided `xor` is a CONSEQUENCE (a one-predecessor exit reached with the value in eax costs 2 bytes, which is what tips cl into duplicating) - give the block a second predecessor and the xor returns; named zero local, (BOOL)0, FALSE, NULL, volatile, `== 0` and an intervening store were all measured and none change it. cl inverts the LAST goto's branch, so the guard you send to `fail` last decides where the shared block lands. Screen: `gruntz walls diagnose`. CSBI_ImageSetAni::Init 0xe7980 88.49 -> 96.43, CSBI_StatzTabGruntBar::BuildMultiplayerTabStatusBar 0xea1f0 88.56 -> 94.68, CSBI_GruntMachine::BuildResourceTabStatusBar 0xe8a70 85.91 -> 93.17, CDDrawSubMgrPages::TransEnter 0x158e40 88.21 -> 97.31. PICK THE SPELLING BY WHERE RETAIL'S SHARED BLOCK SITS: mid-body => `goto`, sunk past the success return => `||` (CPlay::OnLButtonDblClk 0xce660 86.53 -> 90.35 with goto, -> 95.70 with `||`); the label's SOURCE position is irrelevant. The shared block need not be a bare return - five copies of `{ m_defenderState = AISTATE_SEEK; return 1; }` behind one `seek:` label took CGrunt::StepArrivalDefense 0xf2b20 76.85 -> 83.09 and StepArrivalDefenseLean 0xf8240 54.22 -> 63.47. A surplus ret is ALSO a correctness-bug detector: CTriggerMgr::DestroyGroup 0x798d0 86.63 -> 100 EXACT (a `return 0` outside its guard block) and CGrunt::OnStruck 0x588f0 75.48 -> 90.39 (a spurious `else`). NOT this lever when the duplicated block is a /GX unwind epilogue (`mov fs:0,ecx`) - that is a scheduling-driven cross-jump both compilers coin-flip, and six spellings were measured on CLatencyList::FillCombo 0x37ff0 with none better. The MIRROR direction (retail has more exits, 45 functions) is NOT solved and /Os is not a lever (pragma optimize("s") scored 88.49 -> 45.07)

RETRACTED: 'caller SIZE alone does nothing' - that experiment was SATURATED (2 sites, nothing rejected at pad=0, so extra budget could not show). At a rejecting configuration (S=8 callee, 12 sites) caller padding moves it 9/9/9/9/10/12/12 for pad 0/5/10/20/40/80/160 - the plateau is the 1000 floor and it breaks at ~36-40 caller statements. Caller padding never moves it. So the gap is ~6 missing inline call sites in the caller's tail, measured, not a wall. Harness: tools/inline-budget/. /O2 is /Ob1 - cl 5.0 never auto-inlines an unmarked function at any definition position. Devices that DO force the COMDAT are all fitted artifacts and must not be landed (file-static PMF, non-static PMF, `__declspec(dllexport)`); `#pragma auto_inline(off)` does nothing to an explicit inline and `#pragma inline_depth` is ignored by cl 5.0. - [`~SPRITE_STATE_HIDDEN` masks 16 bits, not 32 - MSVC 5.0 promotes a small enum to `unsigned short`](enum-complement-is-sixteen-bit.md) - c10 - cpp:enum cpp:flags cpp:expr | asm:and | topic:codegen-idiom topic:correctness - retail has `and dword ptr [reg+ofs],0xfffffffe` (`83 /4 imm8`, 4 bytes) where the base has `and dword ptr [reg+ofs],0xfffe` (`81 /4 imm32`, 7 bytes) and the source line is `x &= ~SOME_ENUMERATOR;`. - [`new` factories: which arm is the fall-through names the source's if/else shape](allocate-check-then-body-is-the-then-block.md) - c9 - cpp:if cpp:new | asm:jcc | topic:codegen-idiom - a `new X; check; init; AddTail; return` factory stuck at 70-80% whose `gruntz walls diagnose --asm` shows a kind mismatch at the first branch after the allocation: cl5 lays an `if (c) { S }` THEN-block inline after the inverted branch, so `jne <body>` + a full inline epilogue IS `if (p == NULL) return 0;` while `je <shared tail>` is `if (p != NULL) { ... } return 0;`, and `jne <tail at END>` IS `if (p->flag == 0) { body; return p; }` with the teardown trailing. - [`new` factories: which arm is the fall-through names the source's if/else shape](allocate-check-then-body-is-the-then-block.md) - c6 - cpp:if cpp:new | asm:jcc | topic:codegen-idiom topic:wall - a `new X; check; init; AddTail; return` factory stuck at 70-80% whose `gruntz walls diagnose --asm` shows a kind mismatch at the first branch after the allocation. CORRECTED 2026-08-08: the table's ROW 2 (`je <shared tail>`) is what OUR build collapses row-1 source into, NOT an independent source shape - all three named functions branch `jne <body>` in RETAIL, i.e. a real early `if (p == NULL) return 0;`. The gate row is ALSO not independently steerable: flipping between rows 3 and 4 moves the gate polarity and the null-return block in OPPOSITE directions and never lands both (AddToList1 0x116cf0 80.00 teardown-first vs 77.41 body-first; AddToList3Switch 0x116b80 75.78 vs 72.30; both measured twice). A class `new` can also have TWO genuine tests: cl's raw allocation/constructor guard, followed by a source-authored check of the selected constructed pointer. HsvShiftTable 0x14e540, WinMain, and all six WorldSoundSet factories prove the distinction; removing the six explicit checks kept the generated branches but lost one retail return and six exact matches. The constructor-remodel heuristic applies only when a separately proven ctor-like `Init` wrapper is being replaced, not to factory error handling.
- [A hand-carried loop counter/offset LOWERS register pressure - write the plain `for` with the full index](hand-carried-loop-index-lowers-register-pressure.md) - c10 - cpp:loop cpp:for cpp:local cpp:array | asm:or asm:lea asm:push | topic:codegen-idiom topic:regalloc - a counted loop where every diff row says retail spends MORE than we do, and the base comes out SHORTER than retail (154 vs 179, 244 vs 254): retail carries a `push ecx`/`sub esp,N` slot we lack, spills a loop-invariant into it and reloads it per iteration, and picks a register read-modify-write (`mov r,[m]; or r,imm; mov [m],r`) where cl narrows to `or byte [m+2],1`. Cause: a hand-rotated downcount (`n = last-row+1; do{...n--;}while(n)`), a hand-carried cursor (`cell++`) or a hand-carried offset accumulator (`g2 += COLS`) is a NAMED LOCAL, while the same value created by cl's own induction-variable pass is a derived value it will home in a dead PARAMETER slot - so the hand form wins back a callee-saved register, runs one register RICHER than retail, and cl then selects the cheaper encoding. The RMW is a CONSEQUENCE, not a spelling: all five of `|=`, named temp, temp+or, self-or and cached-object compile byte-identically, so rmw-split-means-a-named-temp does not reach this case. Fix: plain `for (r = row; r <= last; r++)` with the FULL index at every use (`m_cellFlag[r*COLS+col]`, `out[n] = item`), no accumulator and no cursor. CTriggerMgr::ClearGridRange 0x6bd40 66.97 -> 100.00 EXACT (64-cell forest, 12 exact, every one plain-for + full index and none with the accumulator), CDDrawPtrCollections::CreateRange 0x142630 84.02 -> 100.00 EXACT (181-cell forest, 12 exact, every one `out[n]`). Both were filed regalloc walls. NOT this lever when the base is LONGER than retail or the `ret` counts differ - that is hand-rotated-loop-merges-the-exit-epilogues
- [A hand-rotated loop merges the two exit epilogues retail keeps apart](hand-rotated-loop-merges-the-exit-epilogues.md) - c9 - cpp:loop cpp:while cpp:for cpp:goto | asm:jcc asm:ret | topic:codegen-idiom - the loop latch reads `jcc <exit>` + `jmp <top>` where retail has one `jcc <top>` falling into the exit, the base has one FEWER `ret`, and retail's loop-exit copy OMITS the `xor eax,eax` the base's shared one has (it knows the cursor is already 0 in eax). Cause: a loop written pre-rotated by hand (`if (pos == NULL) return 0; do {...} while (pos != NULL); return 0;`) hands cl two textually identical `return 0` statements to cross-jump; retail's compiler rotated the loop itself and emits one exit block per exit EDGE, which are not identical so nothing merges. Fix: write `while (pos != NULL) { ... return 1; }` with the hit-body inline, or `for (i = 0; i < N; i++, list++)` - and put BOTH the counter and the cursor bump in the `for` clause (leaving `list++` last in the body emits `add ebx,0x1c` before `inc esi`; retail is the other way and that swap was the last two instructions). CTriggerMgr::RemoveCellRecord 0x78260 84.63 -> 89.59, CTriggerMgr::SelectionListFind 0x7d2a0 84.58 -> 100.00 EXACT, CProjectile::ScanTargets 0xe0b10 95.37 -> 100.00 EXACT. **SCREEN ON THE ASM, NEVER THE SOURCE** (measured tree-wide 2026-08-08): the source shape is retail's OWN idiom - CMapMgr::Find 0x9f500, CSpawnList::ClearFlags 0x9a420, CMenuState::StopMusicChain 0xa0640, CWormhole::SpawnPartners 0x403b0, CAttract::LeaveState 0x14340 and the whole MFC POSITION-walk family are 100.00 EXACT carrying it verbatim, and A/B'ing the transform on every source-matching site scored 1 keep / 8 neutral / 15 WORSE (down to -16 pts). Require BOTH halves of the asm fingerprint at once (base has a lone `[jmp Bk^]` AND target has `[jcc Bk^ | fall ret]`) - that pair fires on exactly 2 functions tree-wide. The separable, still-greppable variant is the PREHEADER one: base N+1 blocks vs target N with a lone small `[fall Bk]` ahead of the loop head (CDDrawWorkerHost::InitGeometry 0x1619f0 94.02 -> 95.89, WwdFile::GetMapBaseName 0x3bb50 83.29 -> 88.24). Trap: deleting the guard while KEEPING the do/while changes the semantics and scores WORSE (82.00 on GetMapBaseName). NOT this lever when retail itself has an explicit top guard AND a preheader (CDDSurface::ShadeBlt 0x13f020) - read `--blocks --lite --target` first
- [A `push <imm>` next to a `mov reg,<imm>; push reg` proves TWO call sites, not one](literal-push-proves-a-second-call-site.md) - c9 - cpp:switch cpp:if cpp:call | asm:push asm:jmp | topic:codegen-idiom - N switch arms are each exactly ONE instruction short (`mov eax,<imm>; jmp <join>` against retail's `mov eax,<imm>; push eax; jmp <join>`) and the else/default path pushes a BARE IMMEDIATE in the target where the base routes it through the same register. The immediate push is the tell: a single call taking a variable would route EVERY path through that variable's register, so a literal on one path and a register on the others means two distinct call sites that cl cross-jumped down to their common tail. Fix: put the call inside both branches (`if (colored) { switch(...) {...} SetTextColor(hdc, color); } else { SetTextColor(hdc, TCLR_WHITE); }`) instead of one call after the if/else. - [A run of adjacent same-value `buf[k]=c` stores is a LOOP](adjacent-same-value-stores-are-a-loop.md) - c10 - cpp:loop cpp:array | asm:mov asm:stos asm:shl | topic:codegen-idiom - retail writes a `u16` array with `mov DWORD PTR [esi+N],eax` at a 2-mod-4 offset after the word-duplication idiom (`mov cx,ax / shl ecx,0x10 / mov cx,ax`); the recompile emits one `mov WORD PTR` per element. `for(i=a;i<b;i++) buf[i]=c;` gets cl5's FILL expansion (rep stos DWORD for long runs, unrolled DWORD stores for short, `+ stos WORD` when the count is ODD); individual assignments never do - alignment is irrelevant, and even a 2-element run is a fill. - [A zero-store GROUP after a call is a LOOP - that is what gives it its own `xor eax,eax`](zero-group-loop-gives-its-own-constant.md) - c9 - cpp:loop cpp:array cpp:ctor | asm:xor asm:mov asm:push | topic:codegen-idiom topic:regalloc - the STEERABLE inverse of zero-register-pinning: base pushes one extra callee-saved register and pins 0 in it, retail emits `xor eax,eax` TWICE (once each side of a call). - [A site that RELOADS a frame slot where you recompute names the constant you invented](reload-instead-of-recompute-names-the-constant.md) - c10 - cpp:const cpp:struct cpp:local | asm:lea asm:add asm:mov | topic:mis-model topic:tooling - a rect/widget builder stuck in the 70s-80s whose `gruntz walls diagnose` is already 100% clean (a wrong CONSTANT changes no branch, no block and no ret): where a site's `arg.top`/`arg.bottom` arrives as `mov eax,[esp+M]` with no arithmetic near it, retail is REPEATING a value an earlier site wrote, so any different constant in your source there is invented. - [A stack slot holding ONE channel of a packed constant is a CSE, not a separate constant](cse-partial-term-is-not-a-separate-constant.md) - c10 - cpp:local cpp:const | asm:sar asm:shl asm:or | topic:mis-model topic:tooling - in a table of composed constants (`Pack(r,g,b)` over shift globals) cl5 CSEs each per-channel term into `[esp+N]` and ORs it into a LATER constant; read top-down that slot looks like a finished constant with two channels zero, so the transcription invents an extra constant AND zeroes that channel in every real one. No class shares another's vtable RVA → dissolve the ~65 RELOC_VTBL + L_/S_ placeholders to real per-class vtables; sharing a sibling's = UB. 8-experiment toolchain proof + repro.
- [Non-virtual header-inline member craters includers (delinker per-unit packing)](nonvirtual-inline-header-craters-delinker-packing.md) — c8 — cpp:inline cpp:method | topic:wall topic:tooling — moving a scattered NON-virtual multi-caller method into its class header (its true home) emits a COMDAT in every includer-caller; the delinker attributes that rva to the CALLER's unit and packs it into that unit's target .text → craters the caller's real fns (ComputeCellFlags→Brickz.h: BuildRockBreak 81→0, ApplyMove 70→0, ComputeCellFlags LOST). - [An `RVA()` extent must include the function's own switch TABLES](rva-extent-must-include-switch-tables.md) - c10 - data:objdiff cpp:switch | asm:jmp | topic:scoring-artifact topic:tooling - a function whose disasm is byte-identical (same instruction count, every branch displacement equal) is stuck below 100 and its base .text COMDAT is much longer than the RVA() size: MSVC5 emits a switch's jump table AND byte index table INSIDE the function's own COMDAT, so objdiff scores our table bytes against retail's NEXT function. Test without disassembling: COMDAT size - claim > 15, and the first `$L` LABEL symbol (scl 6) sits within 3 bytes of the claim (the table's 4-byte alignment) - that proves our code ends where retail's does, so the new extent IS the COMDAT size. Tree sweep 2026-08-01: 43 of 43 improved, nothing else moved, 23 straight to EXACT incl. CPlay::BuildGruntTypeNameTable 33.33 -> 100. Overall 3214 -> 3240 exact. Traps: a section can hold >1 function (bound at the next fn symbol) and RVA() is sometimes INDENTED in a namespace. SUPERSEDED DETECTOR: read RETAIL instead - from the claim end, 4-align, count DWORDs landing inside [rva, rva+size], then bytes < that count, and REPEAT (functions carry several switches); confirm each by requiring the body to literally contain the table's VA. That needs no base obj, so it also finds functions emitting NO table - where the short claim was INFLATING the score. Second sweep 2026-08-01: 65 confirmed / 0 rejected, 38 applied, MAX 81.73 -> 82.30; it refutes this entry's own `slop > 3 means real work` bound (_EngStr_RenderText 61.40 -> 90.55) and caught 3 multi-table fns (HandleCommand 3 tables +1551, 71.16 -> 94.70). A DROP after extending is not a revert signal if the body references the table VA - it means our source emits no table (see switch-empty-arms-dedup-before-jumptable.md)
- [An `RVA()` extent must not run PAST the function into the linker's `0xCC` filler](rva-extent-must-not-run-into-linker-filler.md) - c10 - data:objdiff cpp:switch | asm:int3 asm:jmp | topic:scoring-artifact topic:tooling - the MIRROR of the short-claim artifact and worth more: retail was linked /INCREMENTAL, so the linker leaves a growth gap of up to 2 KB of 0xCC BETWEEN functions, and a claim that over-runs into it hands objdiff hundreds of `int3` with nothing on the base side. cl pads a .text COMDAT to 16 with 0x90, so 0xCC inside a claim is PROOF the claim is too long; the extent ends at the first 0xCC run of >=8 before the next admitted function. CGrunt::LoadGruntTypeTable 0x4dd50 carried 1168 filler bytes - 1152 int3 against a 3707-instruction target - and went 59.76 -> 87.11 on the size arg alone (it was the largest residual body in the tree). Also CMapMgr::ComputeCellFlags 46.04 -> 76.40, CInGameIcon::CInGameIcon 79.11 -> 92.23, CGrunt::WanderStep 66.71 -> 83.44. A long claim MASKS a short one, so fix this direction FIRST. Screen: `gruntz sema disasm --switch` (0/3455 false positives on the byte-exact set, both directions)
- [cl cross-jumps a switch arm's 10-14 byte tail out of ~1 arm in 3; retail duplicates it](switch-arm-tail-crossjump-vs-duplicate.md) - c9 - cpp:switch cpp:branch | asm:jmp asm:or | topic:wall topic:regalloc - MOST arms of a big per-case switch are byte-identical to retail but a PERIODIC subset (every 2nd/3rd) is exactly 10 or 14 bytes SHORT and ends `mov edi,<imm>` + a jmp landing a few bytes BEFORE where its siblings jump: cl's tail-merge pass hoisted its last two instructions into one shared copy. The arms it picks follow the /O2 register rotation (eax/ecx/edx cycling across consecutive identical arms), so nothing in the C++ distinguishes a merged arm from an unmerged one. Second face: whether an arm's `flags |= 0x10` is the mergeable `or DWORD PTR [m],0x10` (7 B) or retail's `mov eax,[m] / or al,0x10 / mov [m],eax` (14 B) is decided by whether EAX is free, and EAX is taken exactly when cl CSE'd the shared constant 1 into it rather than into EBX as retail did - one register choice flips instruction selection, which flips mergeability, which flips ~20 tails at once. Measured: CGrunt::LoadPickupSprites 0x65e80 (18 of 60 arms short by 10), CGrunt::LoadGruntTypeTable 0x4dd50 (12 of 20 or-sites), and it is the mechanism behind CTriggerMgr::ResetGroup 0x79520, which has a clean extent and a clean dispatch shape. RETRACTED for CStatusBarMgr::SetTabState 0x100d70 2026-08-08: its arms end in a statement-identical `return 1;`, which is the EARLY return cross-jump, and `break;` + one trailing `return 1;` takes it 88.53 -> 100.00 EXACT - check the arm terminator first. DO NOT read a periodic 10-byte arm deficit as a missing statement
- [N distinct empty `ret` destructor COMDATs mean N distinct CLASSES](distinct-empty-dtor-comdats-are-distinct-classes.md) - c10 - cpp:dtor cpp:eh cpp:class cpp:inline | asm:ret asm:jmp | topic:identity topic:correctness - cl emits ONE dtor COMDAT per type and the retail link has no /OPT:ICF, so two identical `ret`-only destructors are never folded: every distinct empty-dtor ADDRESS the unwind funclets reach is a distinct CLASS. Several members our headers give ONE type whose funclets land on DIFFERENT 1-byte `c3`s = a CONFLATION to split. `eh_band --census` prints `retail -` for an unpinned COMDAT and `FUN_<va>` when it goes through an ILT thunk - both mean unpinned, not missing: resolve the `e9` chain and read the body (`c3` = a header-inline `~X(){}`, `e9 <dtor>` = `~X(){ OneCall(); }`), then `RVA_COMPGEN(<rva>, 0x1|0x5, ??1X@@QAE@XZ)`. `0xcc` fill on BOTH sides proves the body is its own linker contribution, so the host `.cpp` only has to be one whose base obj defines the symbol. DSoundList was THREE retail classes (0x135ba0/0x1364e0/0x1364f0) while its ops exist in one shared copy -> all four directsoundmgr groups unwind-identical; RegistryHelper 0x201f0, CMotionState 0x58ba0 and PlayerLatency 0x832e0 were merely unpinned (7 groups, one line each). Still open: CHash is one modelled type where retail has two (0x139dd0, 0x139ed0)
- [An unwind funclet NAMES the type of the object it destroys - it is a member/base oracle](eh-funclet-names-the-type.md) - c10 - cpp:eh cpp:ctor cpp:dtor cpp:class | asm:jmp asm:call | topic:identity topic:correctness - a wrapper struct with one member, an invented intermediate base, a class-level `operator new`/`operator delete` that only forwards to the global, or a `.cpp`-pinned function whose only retail callers are unwind funclets. cl gives every /GX function one funclet per EH state and each is `mov ecx,[ebp-X]; add ecx,<off>; jmp ??1<Class>` - the destructor it NAMES is a fact about retail's declaration that nothing else states as directly. The CONSTRUCTOR site cannot: a wrapper around one member and the member itself call the same ctor at the same address and are byte-identical there; only the destructor path separates them, because a wrapper gets its own synthesised `??1Wrapper` COMDAT. `gruntz.delink.eh_band --census --top N` histograms `ours -> retail` over the differing funclets, and one row is ONE modelling decision however many TUs repeat it. Caught 2026-08-10, 146 of 750 groups: CUserBaseLink was a wrapper around a plain `zBitVec` member (57, and the frame-offset class fell 105 -> 41 with it); `?RezFreeStdcall@@YGXPAX@Z` 0x853d0 was MFC's `??3CObject@@SGXPAX@Z` - 56 callers, every one a funclet, no game code at all (34); `?Tm_DestroyArray@@YGXPAXHIP6AXXZ@Z` 0x11f640 was cl's vector destructor iterator `??_M@YGXPAXIHP6EX0@Z@Z` (13 funclets + 13 ordinary functions); three class-level `operator delete` forwarders REFUTED by a funclet calling the global (8); and `~WwdRegion{}`/`~WwdDirtyRect{}` needing an `RVA_COMPGEN` pin rather than an out-of-line body, because retail inlines the empty body away at every call site yet the funclet still jumps to its address (18). Compare resolved ADDRESSES, not names, wherever both sides resolve

- [`.bss` sections cap below 100% — objdiff infers COFF symbol sizes from the next symbol's offset](bss-symbol-size-inference-hole.md) — c9 — data:bss data:objdiff | topic:scoring-artifact topic:tooling — RESOLVED 2026-08-09 (objdiff-cli built from source, nix/patches/objdiff-bss-inferred-extent.patch): matched_data 16.83% -> 90.87%, function scoring bit-identical. Mechanism: COFF states a symbol size only for a COMMON symbol, so objdiff INFERS size = next symbol's offset (obj/read.rs infer_symbol_sizes) and diff_bss_symbol scored 100 iff the two agreed - but the base layout is cl's and the target layout is the DELINKER's aligned append, so the span compares two allocators' padding, not the program. Census (gruntz verify data-coverage): 363 paired .bss symbols, 50 disagreements, widest |delta| 4 B. Fix compares sizes only when a side STATES one; unpaired symbols still mismatch. The delinker cannot state a size (COFF has no field) and tight-packing would destroy the section's retail contribution span AND still miss ddsurface, whose 8-byte reading is on the BASE side. Still binding: do NOT candidate-shape .bss (vacuous 100% for ANY globals) and do NOT add filler globals. Residual .bss gap is the $S NAMING gap
- [`.bss` sections cap below 100% — objdiff infers COFF symbol sizes from the next symbol's offset](bss-symbol-size-inference-hole.md) — c9 — data:bss data:objdiff | topic:wall topic:scoring-artifact topic:tooling — a .bss section stalls at 99.99% and matched_data (all-or-nothing per SECTION) credits it 0; the residual is always ONE small int, the one cl put at section offset 0. COFF has no symbol sizes, so objdiff INFERS size = next symbol's offset (obj/read.rs infer_symbol_sizes) and diff_bss_symbol scores 100 iff sizes are equal - and MSVC5's .bss hole-filling allocator always parks a 4-byte int in the pad before the first 8-aligned array, so it measures 8. NOT steerable: 7 cl probes - the layout is invariant under declaration order, use order and a single rename, but sixteen `int g_a00..g_a15` come out `a00 a01 a02 a03 a10 a04 a11 a05 a12 a06 ...`, so it is a HASH WALK OVER THE SYMBOL NAMES and the only lever is renaming globals to fit (a fitted artifact - forbidden). the two sides use DIFFERENT ALLOCATORS - cl's real VC5 rule is now known and written down (docs/compiler-data-layout.md: double/i64 8, other scalars 4, aggregate >8 -> 8, <4 -> 4, 4..8 -> a per-section ratchet) while the target is a synthetic repack in RVA order, so their padding can only agree by luck. RESOLVED both ends: objdiff declines to compare a span neither side states, and the delink manifest calls data_layout.obj_align instead of "8 iff size % 8 == 0" (the ratchet is unrecoverable and stays un-latched at 4). Do NOT candidate-shape .bss (no bytes exist -> 100% by construction for ANY globals = vacuous) and do NOT add filler globals. Fix is upstream (delinker emits sizes / score bss by declared type), not in src/
- [A `.rdata`/`.data` symbol whose target extent is 2-6 bytes long has absorbed the linker's inter-COMDAT padding](delinked-data-extent-absorbs-linker-padding.md) - c9 - data:rdata data:data data:objdiff | topic:wall topic:scoring-artifact topic:tooling - a data section stalls at 99.x% with one SIZE row where target - base is 1..7 on a `??_7`/`??_C@`/`??_R[0-3]` symbol. Every such COMDAT has its EXACT size in the base obj; link.exe aligns the next one and leaves 2-6 dead bytes, the delinker groups retail-adjacent data into ONE section, and objdiff infers size = next symbol's offset - so the first symbol measures its own size PLUS the slack. The `+4` on ten vtables is NOT a missing virtual: read the bytes (0x1f07bc after ??_7CFader@@6B@ is the float 0.001f from fader's own FP pool; 0x1f075c after ??_7InterfaceObject@@6B@ is zero pad). Runs BOTH ways - ?g_defaultZ@@3IB has retail gap 8 and target 4, because that section is a synthetic repack of three non-contiguous .rdata runs packed at 4. NEVER declare the padded extent (a 6-slot vtable, char[32] for 25 chars) - that writes linker slack into the model and hides the identical evidence shape of a real missing slot. PINNING THE BOUNDARY DATUM DOES NOT CLOSE IT (tested): the delinker packs a group by declared size and ignores each symbol's RVA offset inside it, so fader's real, doubly-referenced 0.001f at 0x1f07bc landed at section offset 0x2c instead of 0x14 (98.58 -> 98.65, extent still 24). The actual culprit is an UNPLACED datum - ??_7CObject@@6B@ is withheld from the section manifest as a duplicate name but still enrolled with section_ordinal `-`, and unplaced rows are appended 8-aligned to the unit's first candidate section. Closes only upstream: config/retail/data_vtables.tsv already carries 0x14 for these vtables but labels.py drops the size because data_manifest.py enrolls the vtable too, and feeding both aborts the delink with `duplicate data RVA`. 41 of the 75 sub-100 data sections are this artifact alone (~11 KB of matched_data unreachable from src/)
- [ILT: retail is linked `/INCREMENTAL`, so a vtable slot holds a `jmp` thunk, not the body](ilt-thunk-indirection.md) — c10 — asm:jmp | topic:delinker topic:scoring-artifact topic:reloc-fidelity — a delinked vtable slot names an unrelated class (`~CTriggerMgr`, `GroupAllScored`, `SetupImage`) or `thunk_FUN_...`, and its `.rdata` can never reach 100%. link.exe /INCREMENTAL routes function-ADDRESS refs through a 5-byte `jmp rel32` band at the start of .text (GRUNTZ: rva 0x1005..0x44a8, 2695 entries, `cc` pad both ends). `cl` cannot name a thunk that does not exist until link, so EVERY DIR32 in the original obj named the BODY - delinking must hop through the thunk (nix/patches/vostok-ilt-thunk-resolution.patch). Corollary: NEVER pin `RVA_COMPGEN`/`RVA()` to a vtable slot's stored VALUE - that pins the symbol onto a 5-byte jmp (14 CUserLogic virtuals did exactly this; real bodies are the 0x87d0..0x89f0 cluster). Reverse audit: invented helpers may chase through one ILT entry to an existing function, into an internal shared tail that calls a real member, or through a `call;ret` wrapper whose consumed EAX proves the member return type (`CueVisible`/`GruntPointVisible`/`BoardTest` -> `PointInBounds`; `EntranceApplyFrame` -> `ApplyLookupSprite`; `GruntTileSwitchImpl` -> `StepArrivalDrop`). A callee-cleaned `ret N` does not by itself prove `__stdcall`: ECX use and caller receiver setup exposed `0x11b7c0` as a five-argument member overload. Verify every call site independently: one fake name may hide different retail targets or an entire missing branch (`ApplyBox`). Trap: tiny default bodies draw FID LOW false-positives (`__fpclear` claimed at 4 RVAs at once)
- [A generic library/FID label held by a game vtable is a per-class compiler-generated method](library-fid-label-vtable-xref.md) — c10 — cpp:dtor cpp:vtable cpp:comdat | topic:identity topic:mis-model topic:tooling — isolated library color deep inside a game TU; generic `??_G__non_rtti_object`/MFC sibling name on a common scalar-deleting-dtor byte pattern. Audit: 155/162 generic `??_G__non_rtti_object` rows are proven game-code category errors

- [The `.CRT$XC` table is a COMPLETE census of the image's file-scope constructed objects](crt-xc-table-is-the-static-initializer-census.md) — c10 — cpp:static cpp:ctor | data:layout | asm:jmp asm:mov | topic:identity topic:tooling — a `.text` body with ZERO call sites whose address appears exactly once as an absolute `.data` pointer, usually behind a 0x50-byte cell (`e9 1b 00 00 00` + 11x `0x90` + 16x `0xcc`, body at +0x20). - [A byte tested-and-OR'd around a one-time init is `static T x = <dynamic>;`](function-local-static-dynamic-init-guard.md) - c10 - cpp:static cpp:local cpp:branch cpp:data | asm:test asm:or asm:jne | topic:codegen-idiom topic:identity - a file-scope `u8` flag whose ONLY two uses are `& bit` and `|= bit` around an initializer is MSVC5's dynamic-init guard, not source: one flag BYTE per function, one BIT per static (`LoadScrollSpeedOptions` 0xd12b0 tests `& 1` then `& 2` on 0x24c01c for two statics). - [A `.data` global's owner TU is the object whose COMDAT literals BRACKET it](data-run-owner-from-bracketing-comdats.md) - c9 - data:layout data:attribution | topic:identity topic:tu-partition - retail `.data` is one CONTIGUOUS run per .obj, and inside a run the ordinary words come FIRST and that object's `??_R0`/`??_C@` COMDATs after; so a stray global belongs to the object whose literal block FOLLOWS it (map every `.text` base-reloc into the window back to its containing function to name each block). Write ASSIGN-THEN-OVERRIDE (`i32 cap = level; if (level > 0x16) cap = unit->m_19c;`), never `?:` and never an if/else that assigns in both arms; keep a `flags = 0` seed ABOVE the compare, not inside the else (in the else cl reuses the zeroed register for an unrelated member store where retail writes an immediate). Inverse direction = assign-then-CANCEL (`i32 v = m_19c; if (v != 1) v = 0;`) - every test-first spelling lets cl const-prop the `== 1` into `cmp mem,1` + an immediate. DUAL: which arm retail keeps INLINE names the side the source's `if` tested. SECOND SWEEP 2026-07-27 resolves `b_ret>t_ret` into FOUR distinct spellings (20 more fns moved, 1 EXACT): (1) SEARCH LOOP - hoist the guard, `if(c){do{...}while(c);}`; a plain for/while duplicates the miss epilogue as the loop fall-through where retail branches out and takes an unconditional back-edge (MgrListFind 73.2->89.5, FindStartPointAt 83.2->93.5, CellHitTest 71.2->80.7, FindGruntAt 74.8->79.4 - three of those were filed as regalloc walls); (2) WNDPROC - `break` not `return 0` in a case arm merges it with the switch DEFAULT, parking that block at the bottom and inverting the ladder's last compare (PreDispatchMessage 77.6->98.2, LevelPreviewDlgProc 80.4->85.2, GameOptionsDlgProc 96.1->97.6) - and the dispatch must actually BE a switch, chained `if(msg==X)` is not (ButeAttributezDlgProc needed both: exits alone 27.9->14.9, +switches 100.00 EXACT); (3) MANY GATES - `goto fail` still puts the fail block BEFORE the success tail (2 epilogues); carrying the value (`i32 ok=0; ... ok=1; done: return ok;`) is what gives ONE (LoadGameAssetNamespaces 86.2->88.5) but is NOT universal (same edit on LevelPreviewDlgProc 85.2->79.8, reverted); (4) TWO-ARM STORE at a tail - retail selects the slot pointer and stores once (zPTree::Insert 53.4->60.5, CProjActMap::Insert 58.2->63.6, CWarpStoneFly::Tick 79.4->80.5). - [`#include <new>` silently deletes a /GX unwind frame - use `<new.h>`](nothrow-new-header-kills-gx-frame.md) - c10 - cpp:include cpp:dtor cpp:eh cpp:new | asm:push asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom topic:eh - a /GX destructor is missing retail's WHOLE EH prologue (`push -1 / push handler / mov fs:0,esp / push ecx / mov [esp+4],this / mov [esp+0x10],0`) and no body spelling brings it back. - [A large SDK header in a shared prelude is a cl 5.0 cliff, not a style choice](large-sdk-header-in-a-shared-prelude.md) - c9 - cpp:include msvc5:c1 msvc5:sdk | topic:tu-state topic:correctness topic:wall - cl 5.0 SP3 stops resolving `this`-relative members PART WAY THROUGH one function body (`C2227`/`C2228`/`C2065` on members it accepted a dozen lines earlier, source unchanged) once a big SDK header enters a widely-included header's closure; and/or a broad below-bank sweep lands in units that reference nothing the new header declares. MMNO* knobs do NOT rescue it: `WAVEFORMATEX` lives inside the `MMNOWAVE` section and `<dsound.h>` needs it, and the header's include guard makes the first parser decide for the whole TU. Rule: a big SDK header goes in the `.cpp` that calls into it; detection is the regressing-units-are-non-users ratio, read BEFORE the score

- [A spilled discriminant BLOCKS the `else if` jump-thread: the reload lands in the shared predecessor](spilled-discriminant-blocks-the-elseif-jump-thread.md) - c8 - cpp:if cpp:branch cpp:local | asm:jcc asm:mov asm:test | topic:wall topic:regalloc topic:codegen-idiom - `gruntz walls diagnose` reports EQUAL branch counts and exactly one TOPOLOGY row (one jcc lands one block later in retail) while `--diff` and `gruntz walls diagnose --asm` read identical instruction for instruction. A three-arm `if (a && b) / else if (a) / else if (!b)` dispatch has two exits from the `&&`; on the `a` exit the second if is known true so cl jumps PAST its re-test into the arm body. Retail threads that edge because the discriminant POINTER is live in a register; the recompile threads neither, because its pointer is memory-homed, the third arm needs it in a register, and cl hoists that reload into the block BOTH edges pass through - a target block that now starts with an instruction cannot be entered one instruction later. CDDrawShadeBlit::BlitCopyMirrored 0x149d00 (55/55 branches, one topology row, flat at 79.48 across 106 TU states and every arm-chain spelling). The topology row is a CONSEQUENCE of register homing, so no if/else-if/nesting/goto respelling reaches it - read it as a homing report, not a control-flow bug
- [An include's POSITION picks a function's register scheme (cl 5.0)](string-h-intrinsics-reallocate-the-tu.md) - c9 - cpp:include | asm:mov asm:reg | topic:codegen-idiom topic:regalloc topic:wall - cl 5.0 picks a function's register assignment partly from a TU-cumulative count of file-scope DECLARATIONS parsed so far. Declarations above a function rotate it; declarations BETWEEN two functions rotate only the second. Blowfish_encipher/decipher are near-twins that retail compiled into different schemes: `<memory.h>` above both gives 100.00/61.64, `<string.h>` above both 60.41/100.00, `<string.h>` BETWEEN them 100.00/100.00 (whole unit exact). The window is one declaration wide (N=4 dummy prototypes hits it, N=3 and N=5 miss). NOT the intrinsic set - MSVC5 STRING.H has no `#pragma intrinsic`. NOT expression spelling - 8 equal spellings of the round macro are byte-identical. Bodies do not count, only declarations. When two near-twins in one TU cannot both be exact, put the declarations between them

Distinct-caller count is a routing hint, not a source oracle; require the actual callee referent and independent class/ownership evidence.

- [Enum domains: MSVC5 sizes every enum as `int`, but `i32` -> enum is BYTE-NEUTRAL](enum-domains.md) - c10 - cpp:enum cpp:switch | asm:mov asm:cmp | topic:codegen-idiom - measured on VC5.0 SP3: `enum class` and `enum E : u8` do NOT parse (C2236/C2059) and `sizeof(enum)` is ALWAYS 4, so a 1-byte field can never be enum-typed; but retyping a member/param/return/switch-key from `i32` to a real enum and naming the literals leaves `.text` byte-identical - only a signature's mangling moves (`H` -> `W4Kind@@`). - [`mov [this+N],<zero reg>` where retail RE-READS the member it just tested against NULL](branch-equality-propagated-into-the-guarded-store.md) - c8 - cpp:if cpp:member cpp:assign | asm:cmp asm:mov | topic:wall topic:regalloc - `if (p->f == NULL) { m_x = p->f; }` - after `cmp [p+N],<zeroreg> / jne` our cl5 records the equality and stores the zero register, retail re-loads `[p+N]` inside the branch though it also has a zero in a register. Exactly one instruction short; a local for the value, a local for the receiver, `!p->f`, and an inline helper taking the receiver all fail to move it (re-spelling through the other member holding the same pointer defeats it but overshoots by a pointer load). Expensive indirectly: the extra use of 0 can tip cl into homing a callee-saved zero register retail lacks (`push ebx / xor ebx,ebx`), which is what pins CFrontCandyAni 0xacf40 at 49.3. Also CAniCycle 0xaad20, CEyeCandy 0xac620
- [`cmp reg,reg` where ours has `cmp [mem],reg`: the comparison's LEFT operand was a NAMED TEMPORARY](compare-lhs-materialized-before-its-call.md) - c9 - cpp:expr cpp:local cpp:call cpp:if | asm:cmp asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom - `x->field >= f(...)` gives `call f / cmp [reg+disp],eax` in ours and `mov edi,[reg+disp] / call f / cmp edi,eax` in retail, so the function is one instruction SHORT per comparison and the masked diff shows only what looks like an operand-mode choice. cl5's operand evaluation order is not fixed by the source, but a named temporary IS a sequenced statement - hoist each comparison's LHS. The second arm of a `||` has no statement position, so it needs `(t = expr) >= f(...)`. Seven candy/animation ctors share the big-act guard verbatim: CFrontCandy 95.15->99.83, CBehindCandy 92.47->99.83, CDoNothing 94.68->99.53, CSimpleAnimation 95.15->98.72, CEyeCandy 94.51->97.85, CEyeCandyAni 93.58->96.60
- [Count the relocation MULTISET before you read its ordered diff](reloc-multiset-before-reloc-sequence.md) - c10 - topic:method topic:triage topic:mis-model | asm:call | cpp:call - `insn_seq --seq` must produce an ALIGNMENT, so a wrong callee is reported as a MOVE: it pairs our extra `A` against retail's extra `B` and narrates block placement, which no amount of statement reordering can fix. A `collections.Counter` over each side's relocation symbols is order-free and answers the stronger question - does retail even call this? Read the rows as: (1) `tgt=0`/`base=0` = a callee that exists on one side only (wrong callee, or a whole statement missing) - fix first; (2) a complementary pair summing equal = a rename/overload swap, which names the replacement for free; (3) off-by-one on a repeated callee = a cross-jumped or duplicated arm; (4) only then the ordered diff. CPlay::Render had been read as pure block placement for sessions; the multiset showed LoadDestructButtonSprite tgt=0/base=3 against LoadMainStatusBarSprite tgt=4/base=1 and FilterList2 tgt=2/base=4 against StepGridWalk tgt=3/base=1, and the surplus `_g_frameDelta` fell out of the same swap (the wrong callee took an argument the right one does not). Measured: CPlay::Render 71.84 -> 76.06, CBattlezMapConfig::ResolveArrival 56.73 -> 82.34, CGrunt::UpdateArrival's tail (SetEntrancePos where retail calls CTriggerMgr::ApplyTriggerA)
- [The ordered RELOC SEQUENCE names the missing STATEMENT in a -200-instruction function](reloc-sequence-diff-names-the-missing-statement.md) - c10 - topic:method topic:triage | asm:call | topic:codegen-idiom - `gruntz walls diagnose <rva>`: keep only the instructions carrying a relocation, as (mnemonic, symbol) pairs in program order, and difflib the two sides. That list IS the function's statements and it survives regalloc and block scheduling. A long unbroken `+` run = a block retail has and you do not (reconstruct it, do not tune); a `-`/`+` pair on one row = a wrong callee or global; a group moved as a unit = block PLACEMENT, i.e. a source-shape idiom. Per-SYMBOL counts find a deleted switch arm faster than the diff. Two false alarms: the two objs can NAME the same address differently (pooled 1-char literals get a neighbour's name - resolve the ADDRESS), and `$T...` vs a named double is the FP constant pool. Measured: TryTeleportToCell 42.21 -> 92.99, AdvanceMotion 64.14 -> 79.03, ScanNearestTarget 51.30 -> 65.71
- [A jump-table switch lays its arms out in SOURCE order - read the tables to recover it](switch-arm-layout-order-is-source-order.md) - c10 - cpp:switch topic:codegen-idiom topic:method | asm:jmp - Unpack the `movb <idx>` / `jmpl *<jt>(,%ecx,4)` tables straight out of the image; sorting the DISTINCT arm targets by address gives retail's `case` order, because cl emits case bodies in source order, not value order. LoadTileArrivalFx's tables said SHOVEL/GAUNTLETZ/GOOBER/SPY/BRICK/TOOB and its nested brick switch said RED/GOLD/BLUE/BLACK - both different from what we had guessed. Also: MSVC5 emits ONE unique-target entry per CONTIGUOUS run of case values, so two adjacent entries holding the same address is a normal `case a: case b: case d:` group, NOT two bodies that got merged - splitting them emits the body twice. Measured 45.15 -> 61.67

Do not hand-tune the ladder

- [A gate that always `return`s lets cl DELETE a later re-test of the SAME storage](always-returning-gate-dce-kills-a-later-switch-arm.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:switch cpp:dce | asm:cmp asm:jne | topic:codegen-idiom - a whole `case` arm is ABSENT from the base obj (the dispatch falls straight into the arm's failure tail) and the reloc-name histogram shows one complete cluster of callees each short by exactly one. cl5 /O2 propagates "this storage is 0" out of a guard all of whose exits `return`, so a later `if (m_x != 0)` on the same member is folded away. Retail emits both because the GATE reads a local (one cached load, several `cmp reg,0`) while the arm re-reads the MEMBER - cl does not propagate across the two storage kinds. Fix: `i32 v = m_x; if (v != 0) {...return;}` at the gate, `if (m_x != 0)` in the arm. Both halves needed; either alone still deletes the arm. CGrunt::ScanNearestTarget 52.70 -> 64.78
- [A per-function MNEMONIC HISTOGRAM (base vs target) names the wrong idiom in one shot](mnemonic-histogram-diff-finds-the-wrong-idiom.md) - c9 - topic:method topic:triage | asm:cdq asm:or asm:fidiv | topic:codegen-idiom - when the instruction COUNT says a bug exists but the stream is too long to read, count each mnemonic per function on both sides and print only the differing opcodes. A wrong source idiom appears as a PAIR of rows - one family we over-emit, one we under-emit: `cltd 0->4 / sarl 8->4` = hand-rolled abs vs the intrinsic; `orw 16->8 / orl 0->11` = a `static_cast<u16>` forcing a 16-bit OR chain; `shll 10->0 / movb 11->0 / orb 5->0` = we inline an LCG retail CALLS (`rand()` vs the inline `GameRand()`); `retl 16->12 / popl 64->48` = four early returns retail tail-merged. Calibrate on a 100% function (histogram must be IDENTICAL); ignore `addb/aas/hlt/sldtw/lretl` on the target side - that is the in-.text jump table disassembled as garbage. Four of five wins in one 40-75 batch came from this

- [Empty `break` switch arms de-dup BEFORE the jump-table decision - spell them `return <v>`](switch-empty-arms-dedup-before-jumptable.md) - c9 - cpp:switch cpp:return | asm:jmp asm:cmp | topic:codegen-idiom - retail has a dense `add eax,-lo / cmp / ja / jmp [eax*4+T]` over a contiguous range where only 2-3 arms do anything, ours emits a `sub/je - dec/je - sub/jne` ladder over just the active values. cl5 collapses identical (empty `break`) arms BEFORE the density test, so 6 labels with 4 empty ones are already a 2-label switch; give each inactive arm its OWN `return <same value>;` and the table comes out (its 6 entries share 3 targets - the merge happens after). - [/Od `[ebp-N]` slots are ordered by the local's NAME (hash), not decl position - SOLVED](od-local-slot-ordering.md) - c9 - cpp:local | asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom topic:regalloc - /Od fn recompiles byte-identical in mnemonics/operands/frame-size but the locals map to a permuted set of the same stack slots (~85% byte / 99.5% fuzzy). - [One extra branch in a loop: cl rotates the loop CONDITION, not the first in-body `break`](loop-rotates-on-the-CONDITION-not-the-first-break.md) - c9 - cpp:loop cpp:branch | asm:jmp asm:cmp | topic:codegen-idiom - `gruntz walls diagnose` says base has exactly ONE more conditional branch, and it duplicates a test that also sits at the top of the loop; retail's back-edge jumps to that top test, ours jumps past it into the body. A two-exit loop (`while (A) { if (B) break; ... }`) has two semantically identical spellings and cl5 rotates only the CONDITION - peeling it as the entry guard and making the bottom test the back-edge - so whichever test you make the condition is the one that gets DUPLICATED, and the one you leave as a `break` becomes the loop head the back-edge lands on. Read it off `--blocks --lite --target`: the test inside the back-edge block IS the condition. HAND-ROTATING DOES NOT WORK (`if (A) do { if (B) break; ... } while (A);` still duplicates B) - only promoting the right test to the condition moves it, which is also why the earlier "while/for(;;)/do-while are byte-identical" negative is consistent (they all keep the same test as the condition). Font wrap trio MeasureWrapped 88.75->90.39 / LayoutWrapped 86.66->89.02 / DrawWrapped 75.21->76.33, all three branch-identical. Sibling form: the peeled entry guard already TRANSCRIBED into the C++ (`if (*p != NULL) { while (i < N) { p++; i++; if (!*p) break; } }`) - delete the hand-written guard and promote its test to the condition; sieve bucket is OTHER not POLARITY because the guard and the back-edge trade test KINDS. CTriggerMgr::SpawnGrunt 0x7c110 74.49->80.31, both rows closed

- [The per-function reference COUNT names the cached global the diff cannot show](global-reference-count-sieve.md) - c9 - cpp:local cpp:global | asm:mov | topic:tooling topic:regalloc - `gruntz walls global-refs` compares the multiset of DIR32 (or `--rel32`) relocation targets per function, base against target, over the normalized objs. Four filters take it to 0 false positives over 4301 paired functions, and each was a family the first draft reported as a finding: window each side at its OWN next symbol (clamping the target to the base's length hides retail's tail in the 213 functions where our body is shorter), canonicalize the `$Sdata_data_<hash>` local-static suffix, drop an addend PAST THE END of the symbol it names (the delinker's unsized-datum fallback - but bound by extent, not zero, or `_g_rasterEdgeL+0x14` reads as a different reference from `+0`), and drop a name only one side mentions in the function. NEVER read a raw per-function reloc list - three of the four families reappear instantly (`"LightFx"` prints as `DemoMover+0xc`). Rows come in three shapes: a cached global, a tail-merge that went the other way, or a ctor/dtor cl inlined and retail did not. Found a zero CTime in every snapshot header
- [The ordered RELOC-SYMBOL sequence names a wrong callee that no masked diff can show](reloc-sequence-diff-finds-wrong-referents.md) - c9 - cpp:call cpp:switch cpp:string | asm:call asm:push | topic:tooling topic:mis-model - `--diff`/`gruntz walls diagnose --asm` mask address operands and `gruntz walls diagnose` only covers branch targets, so a WRONG CALLEE, a wrong global, a rotated set of string arms, a missing call or a wrong argument count is invisible in all three. - [A wrong literal CONSTANT costs ~ONE INSTRUCTION - too little to find by score, so diff the immediates](immediate-multiset-diff-finds-wrong-constants.md) - c8 - cpp:global cpp:switch | asm:cmp asm:and asm:imul | topic:tooling topic:correctness topic:mis-model - a function scores high and is still wrong at run time, or sits in the 70s while every structural check is clean (blocks align, `gruntz walls diagnose` AGREES, callee multiset matches, store_offsets quiet, reloc addends quiet). The defect is a bare number - a mask, divisor, shift count, buffer size or packed tag - and no existing sieve covers non-relocated literals: `--diff` DISPLAYS large immediates as `<addr>` (hard to read off, though objdiff DOES score them), store_offsets sees where not what, and the reloc-addend sieve only sees relocated operands. MEASURED 2026-08-10, correcting an earlier 'invisible to every score' claim in this row: objdiff masks RELOCATED operands, not bare immediates, so a wrong literal IS charged - as one instruction's arg mismatch. A/B on ??0CGrunt with WWD_OBJECT_TYPE_GRUNT wrong (0x100000) vs right (0x1000): 90.3911 vs 90.3936, i.e. 0.0025 pp. Negligible, NOT invisible - below the noise floor, gated by nothing, indistinguishable from regalloc churn, so the score cannot FIND it. llvm-objdump prints AT&T here, so the extraction is exact rather than heuristic: an immediate is precisely a `$`-prefixed literal, while a memory displacement (`0x14(%ebx)`) and a branch target (`0x58b <$L27601>`) never are. `gruntz walls diagnose --asm [--min-pct N] [--unit U]` diffs the per-function multiset; it subtracts relocated immediates (by asking whether a relocation lands inside the instruction, NEVER by a magnitude cut-off, which drops genuine large constants) and esp frame adjusts. THE EXPENSIVE FALSE POSITIVE: `andl $0xff` and `movzbl` are the same `(u8)x` and only the first carries an immediate, so a one-sided 0xff/0xffff is usually a narrowing BOTH sides do - deleting the three `static_cast<u8>` behind GammaTable 0x14e9f0's `OURS-ONLY 0xff x3` took it 94.07 -> 86.29 and the same edit took GreyTable 0x14eef0 92.86 -> 89.14. Also benign: a strength-reduced loop bound (a pointer compare against `&g[256]` reads as a one-sided 0x200, Blit168) and a cross-jump (retail stores 0xbb9 twice in SetGeometry 0x1644a0, cl merges to one; the explicit `default:` does not split them). What survives is the real class - retail's four `$0x60a0a0a` in CTileActionEvent::MorphByTool 0x113420 (98.66%) and `$0x5020501` in CTriggerMgr::ApplySwitch 0x6d300, constants our source contains nowhere. 107 rows at >= 90% on the 2026-08-10 tree
- [A MISSING call site is invisible to every % view - diff the per-function CALL COUNT](missing-call-site-is-invisible-to-every-percent-view.md) - c10 - cpp:call cpp:virtual | asm:call | topic:correctness topic:tooling - the function scores in the 80s-90s, `--diff`/`gruntz walls diagnose --asm`/`gruntz walls diagnose` all report the surrounding code as matching, and the game still faults or silently skips an initializer. Every % view scores the instructions that ARE there; a whole missing call site is scored as nothing. The VIRTUAL form is worst: `call dword ptr [edx+0xa8]` carries NO relocation, so `insn_seq --seq` cannot see it either. CPlay::LoadByMode 0xca200 never issued retail's `mov edx,[esi] / push ebp / mov ecx,esi / call [edx+0xa8]` = CPlay::BuildWorldLevelPath, i.e. the WWD level was never loaded, and the crash landed 0x400 bytes later on a NULL m_mainPlane; the method was defined and reachable only through the vtable so nothing referenced it and no gate noticed (83.84 -> 87.45 once restored, with the six asset loaders taking retail's `reload` argument instead of literal 1 and the BuildWarlordNameTable guard testing `diff` not `modeFlag`). TOOL: `build/callcount_detector.py` diffs per-function call counts over the objdiff base/target obj pairs, bucketed direct/indirect/import/tail; `--slots` gives the VTABLE-SLOT multiset diff (exclude esp/ebp displacements - those are stack fn-ptr slots whose offset tracks the FRAME SIZE). ADJUDICATE BY CALLEE NAME, NOT COUNT: the dominant class is cl CROSS-JUMPING two argument-identical call sites that retail kept apart (CGrunt::UpdateArrival, CWwdSpatialMgr::Relocate 4 RemoveAll sites -> 1, CCheatMgr::LoadCheatConfig, CFaderShape::RenderFrame, CInGameIcon ctor 7x SetupSprite - all benign), then inline-budget divergence (CUserLogic::AttachToObject, CMenuItem::Reset, zPTree::Reset, the CFecFile/CArray COMDATs in ChangeState), and only then a real hole. If every retail-only callee already appears in that function's source it is NOT a defect

TOOLS: the archive-slot I/O sieve (retired), the LogicTypeId factory map (retired), the savegame decoder (retired). MEASURED 2026-08-10: 256 functions carry archive-slot calls and every sequence is identical to retail, so a format desync is EXCLUDED. Companions, also clean: CSnapshotHeader (0x156020 stores CountActive at header+0x110 and g_wwdObjIdCounter at +0x114, size 0x120), WwdSnapshot (0x15ad30 reads 0xa0 then uses +4 id / +8 classid / +0x14 worker name), and the SerialObjectFactory type-id map - read the 69 dwords at RVA 0xeb10, take each arm's LAST `mov [esi],<imm>` before its `mov eax,1`, resolve via config/retail/data_vtables.tsv: all 69 arms match our `case LOGIC_*: new C*;` list. AND READ THE SHIPPED .sav FILES - they refute what code reading cannot: a COMPLETE 0x3843a-byte preview BMP at the tail of SlotN.sav proves ChainForward/SaveScreenshot/SaveFile ran to the end and THAT SAVE SUCCEEDED (so an error message beside complete files came from a different attempt), and Gruntz.sav decoding to a checksum-valid index with the right names and SlotN.sav paths refutes "the slot record was never written". savegame_dump also names the crashing grid cell by resolving the CTriggerMgr grid record (60 ids then m_rowCount[4]; match PER ROW, the neighbouring offsets pass a sum-only test) through the WwdSnapshot table. A clean sieve reframes the bug as a runtime-state guard, and explains the crash SHAPE: CTriggerMgr::Load fills m_grid during BroadcastCmd(SERIAL_LOAD), which RestoreChildren runs BEFORE Deserialize, and Deserialize is what runs CWapX::Chain - so a restore that aborts in that window leaves grunts with a zero m_wwdObject and the next teardown faults in ClearGridRange+0x5d. The fault site is the failure path, not the bug; retail has no NULL guard there and neither should we

RULE: decide inlines-on/off by WHICH TU's address range the COMDAT falls in, never by the delinked section; and run `ninja candidate` before believing any change to a shared MFC wrapper

- [An "unoptimized-codegen wall" is a `#pragma optimize("", off)` region](pragma-optimize-off-region.md) - c9 - cpp:pragma | asm:ebp asm:idiv - one or two ADJACENT functions stuck at 30-60% in an otherwise-100% TU, noted as a "compile-profile wall": retail compiled them UNOPTIMIZED (ebp frame + shared jmp epilogue, parameter reloaded off [ebp+8] every use, `mov ecx,100 / idiv ecx` with NO magic-number strength reduction, every x87 intermediate spilled to [ebp-N]) while the neighbours are frameless/strength-reduced. - [A `switch`'s arm BODIES come out in SOURCE order - read the retail index table to recover it](switch-arm-emission-follows-source-order.md) - c10 - cpp:switch | asm:jmp asm:cmp | topic:codegen-idiom topic:tooling - a switch-heavy function stuck in the high 90s with a huge one-sided `--diff` hunk starting at `jmp dword ptr [reg*4]` (the BASE side is truncated there, and llvm-objdump's per-symbol listing restarts at every `$L` too - read the whole SECTION). cl5 emits arm bodies in source order while the dispatch is value-ordered, so a permutation shows up only as two bodies swapped.
