Corollary: `if (a==X || a==Y) {body}` shares one block (`cmp X; je <shared>`) where retail has `cmp X; jne` + a FULL duplicate body - that is two separate else-if arms in the source (CPlay::LoadWarlordSprites 95.26 -> 99.41). A grouped empty-case `break` can also fold all but the first label into the default slot even when machine targets agree; an explicit shared-exit `goto` kept cases 15-18 on one retail arm and took CTileTriggerLogic::LoadBridgeMove 99.9887 -> 100 EXACT. The table ALSO settles which BODY belongs to which case: CStatusBarMgr::ClearTabGroup had all five arms permuted (m_statObj cleared on GRUNTZ where retail clears it on STATZ) - a behaviour bug worth 0.1 of a point, so only the table catches it. Order alone on its TU sibling UpdateStatusBarTabHighlight (7 arms, tab 0,5,1,4,2,3,6 vs our ascending) 55.34 -> 85.03
- [`dec <reg>` where you wrote `cmp <reg>,1` - the source is a ONE-ARM `switch`, not an `if`](one-case-switch-biases-to-dec-not-cmp.md) - c10 - cpp:switch cpp:if cpp:branch | asm:dec asm:sub asm:cmp asm:jne | topic:codegen-idiom - a tiny handler is byte-for-byte right except its single guard: `--diff` replaces exactly one pair, `-cmp eax,0x1` against `+dec eax`, same jne, same branch and ret counts, so it reads as a register/scheduling wall at 90-99%. cl5 biases EVERY switch subject by the lowest case value before testing anything (the same `value - min` the jump table needs), and the bias survives the DEGENERATE switch - one case, no table, no range check still gets the subtract, encoded `dec` when the case is 1. An `if` never does: `cmp` is non-destructive and the choice is made in the front end from the statement form, not in the encoder, so no /O2 peephole folds one into the other. Read it as a fixed map: `cmp <reg>,0x1; jne` <- `if (x == 1)`, `dec <reg>; jne` <- `switch (x) { case 1: }`, `sub <reg>,N; jne` <- `switch (x) { case N: }`. CMultiStartDlg::OnTimer 0xc2c80 94.00 -> 100.00 EXACT on the statement form alone. Prefer the switch wherever the subject is a DOMAIN (message id, timer id, mode tag) rather than a boolean-ish test - MFC OnTimer/OnCommand bodies are where this shape lives
- [A condition that must survive a destructor call keeps its STATIC TYPE - `!= 0` costs four instructions](condition-saved-across-a-dtor-normalizes-if-spelled-bool.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:eh cpp:local | asm:neg asm:sbb asm:test | topic:codegen-idiom - `mov ebx,eax / neg ebx / sbb ebx,ebx / neg ebx` before a dtor call and `test bl,bl` after it, where retail has a bare `mov ebx,eax` and `test ebx,ebx`. Only exists because of the intervening call, which is why it reads as an EH/regalloc wall. CState::BuildAssetNamespacePrefixes 95.57 -> 100 EXACT on three sites
- [A `?:` with a null arm lays its blocks in CONDITION polarity - an `if/else` does not](ternary-null-arm-order-follows-the-condition-polarity.md) - c9 - cpp:ternary cpp:branch | asm:jcc asm:xor asm:jmp | topic:codegen-idiom - the two arms of a null-guarded ternary come out in the opposite order from retail with identical instructions on both sides. `a != NULL ? f() : NULL` emits the call arm first; `a == NULL ? NULL : f()` emits the zero arm first (retail's shape here). The `?:` knob is the polarity; an if/else has a DIFFERENT knob - inverting it is byte-identical, but splitting the `||` into an else-if chain that spells the zero arm once per test makes cl tail-merge the two blocks and give the merged one the fall-through (CStatusBarMgr::Deserialize 98.11 -> 99.21, branch sequences AGREE). An EIP in a vtable is never a transfer TARGET - decode the vtable words as instructions (`00 00` is `add byte ptr [eax],al`) and the only entry that fits is the vtable BASE, because the one `.rdata` address a live program computes is a vptr. wine's `unsafe_impl_from_IDirectDrawSurface` does `mov edx,[eax]; cmp edx,&ddraw_surface1_vtbl; call DWORD PTR [edx]`, so a surface argument whose first dword is another OBJECT pointer dispatches into that object's `??_7`. Control: `run.sh retail` logs 0 access violations where the candidate logs ~1400, and wine's krnl386 vectored handler returns EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_EXECUTION on most of ours, so one visible crash hides hundreds of silently-continued faults
- [Two fields holding ONE garbage pointer is a stepped-over load, not aliased data - the value is the BASE, not the element](continue-on-fault-retains-the-base-register.md) - c9 - cpp:class cpp:local | asm:mov | topic:runtime topic:identity - two different array indices, or two unrelated struct fields, observed holding the same impossible pointer. wine's krnl386 handler returns EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_EXECUTION and STEPS THE FAULTING INSTRUCTION OVER, so a load that faults never writes its destination and the register keeps the value it held a moment earlier. cl 5.0's indexed read is `mov edx,[ecx+0x14]` (the container's m_pData) then `mov edx,[edx+N]`, so when the second load faults every index collapses onto m_pData - the observation constrains the CONTAINER, not its contents. - [A reloc-ADDEND row is usually a NAME split, not a wrong element - resolve to the address](addend-row-is-a-naming-split-resolve-the-address.md) - c9 - data:objdiff cpp:global cpp:array cpp:loop | asm:cmp asm:lea | topic:tooling topic:scoring-artifact - `reloc_addends` reports "same symbol, different offsets" but the two instructions are IDENTICAL after relocation. A reference is `(symbol, addend)` and the two sides split it differently: cl names what the source named, the delinker names whatever symbol STARTS at the address (or invents `DAT_<rva>`). The commonest producer is a one-past-the-end loop sentinel - `cmp edi, g_battlezLastMaxGruntz+0x10` IS retail's `81 ff 10 9d 62 00`, which delinks as `g_savedDlgWndProc+0`; likewise `g_battlezLastDifficulties+0x10 == g_battlezLastMaxGruntz+0`, and an interior cursor bias `g_levelMsgRectsB+0xc` delinks as `DAT_0020b904`. TWO OF FOUR tree-wide rows were this (50% false-positive rate). Fix is in the tooling, never the source: resolve both sides to an absolute RVA (`build/gen/bindings.tsv` plus the rva embedded in `DAT_`/`$gap_` names), subtract the per-function address multisets, and only an addend landing in the DIFFERENCE is a defect - now class `NAMING`, adjudicated per addend (LevelMsgHudDriver's `+0x80` sentinel pairs away, retail's `+0xc` does not). Unresolvable names (`??_C@`, `__imp__*`, `$Sdata_bss_*`) keep the by-name test and a one-sided resolution stays a defect, so the pass can only remove a false positive

- [A guard's early exit that STILL makes the last call: the tail statement is outside the `if`](guard-exit-that-still-calls-means-a-tail-outside-the-if.md) - c10 - cpp:branch cpp:if cpp:call cpp:stream | asm:pop asm:call asm:jcc | topic:codegen-idiom - the body is byte-identical except the epilogue: retail's leading guard `je`s to a block that still executes the final call and its `pop ebx/ebp/esi` are threaded between that block's own instructions, while ours jumps straight to the `ret`. cl is converging two paths with different push depths - the guarded region owns the extra pushes, so the in-body path pops them BEFORE the join - which means the trailing statement is not inside the `if`. - [A `void*` boundary launders a fake view past every cast ratchet](void-star-is-the-fake-view-laundering-channel.md) - c10 - cpp:cast cpp:class cpp:pointer | topic:correctness topic:tooling - four invented structs survived every gate because a void* splits the dangerous conversion into two LEGAL halves: the producer needs no cast at all (every T* converts to void* implicitly) and the consumer writes ONE static_cast, which is the correct spelling for void* and cannot be ratcheted without flagging correct code. So reinterpret_casts stays 36, the view struct looks used, and each function sees exactly one type per storage. Dissolved 2026-08-18: ScanlinePalette was CImagePaletteNode, SbzDeviceList was CFixedPtrArray32, CRandomAmbientWorld was CDDrawSubMgrLeafScan, MpSymItem was a view of CParseSource - each hidden behind a void* param or return. CSymTab's 13 navigation accessors returning void* hid 79 sites and ~50 casts, all 13 byte-exact once typed. RULE: a void* declaration is an unaudited boundary and its COUNT is a detector metric, not hygiene; retype the declaration and the view dissolves or fails to compile, both informative. Keep void* only at SDK/ABI, allocator, and byte-cursor-to-record boundaries
